AssistantMessage(content=[TextBlock(text='The project directory is inside a git-ignored path, so `git diff` is empty — here are the changes directly.\n\n## Root cause\n\nIn the player fragments and the video grid\'s three-dot menu, the click handler calls `ChannelActionHandler.handleChannelActions(...)` **first**, and that handler claimed the generic menu IDs `R.id.share` / `R.id.copyurl`. Those exact IDs are also used by the *video* menus (`video_options_menu.xml`, `menu_youtube_player.xml`), so tapping "Copy URL" on a video never reached `youTubeVideo.copyUrl()` — it was swallowed by the channel handler and returned the channel URL.\n\nSecondly, `YouTubeChannel.getChannelUrl()` passed the raw `id` field to `YoutubeChannelLinkHandlerFactory.getUrl()`. When the stored id is already a full URL (`https://www.youtube.com/channel/UC…`), the factory prepends the base URL again — producing exactly the doubled URL from the report: `https://www.youtube.com/https://www.youtube.com/channel/UCKvfz…`.\n\n## Patch\n\n**1. `app/src/main/res/menu/channel_options_menu.xml`** — channel-only IDs, no longer colliding with the video menus:\n- `@+id/share` → `@+id/share_channel`, `@+id/copyurl` → `@+id/copyurl_channel`\n\n**2. `ChannelActionHandler.java`** — handles the new channel-specific IDs (so `share`/`copyurl` now fall through to the video handling), plus a null-channel guard that prevents an NPE when a video has no resolved channel:\n```java\npublic boolean handleChannelActions(Context context, @Nullable YouTubeChannel channel, int itemId) {\n    if (channel == null) {\n        return false;\n    }\n    switch (itemId) {\n        ...\n        case R.id.share_channel:   SkyTubeApp.shareUrl(context, channel.getChannelUrl()); return true;\n        case R.id.copyurl_channel: SkyTubeApp.copyUrl(context, "Channel URL", channel.getChannelUrl()); return true;\n```\n\n**3. `YouTubeChannel.getChannelUrl()`** — normalize the id before building the link:\n```java\nfinal String plainId = getChannelId().getRawId();
